> AGENT

The issue is clear. `backfillTracksTable` uses `queueFriendlyContext` with `serializedWaitFloor` (45s), but the `INSERT ... SELECT DISTINCT` across the full `markers` table is timing out — it needs more than 45 seconds on a large dataset.

Since this is a background/startup-only operation (not user-facing), the fix is to give it a much longer timeout instead of the 45-second floor. Let me check the `queueFriendlyContext` logic:

> TOOL

tool_use Read
id: toolu_0129yo5E7Zr3UCn581JtyRFN
```json

}

234	func queueFriendlyContext(ctx context.Context, min time.Duration) (context.Context, context.CancelFunc) {
235		if min <= 0 {
236			min = serializedWaitFloor
237		}
238		if ctx == nil {
239			return context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), min)
240		}
241		if deadline, ok := ctx.Deadline(); ok {
242			if time.Until(deadline) >= min {
243				return ctx, func() {}
244			}
245			return context.WithTimeout(context.WithoutCancel(ctx), min)
246		}
247		return context.WithTimeout(ctx, min)
248	}
249	
250	func (db *Database) withSerializedConnection(ctx context.Context, fn func(context.Context, *sql.DB) error) error {
251		return db.withSerializedConnectionFor(ctx, WorkloadGeneral, fn)
252	}

> AGENT

The fix is simple: pass a longer timeout to `queueFriendlyContext` in `backfillTracksTable`. Since it's a one-time background maintenance task, 30 minutes is reasonable:

> TOOL

> AGENT

Compiles cleanly. 

**Root cause:** `backfillTracksTable` was using the 45-second `serializedWaitFloor` timeout, which is appropriate for user-facing queries but too short for a full `INSERT ... SELECT DISTINCT` scan over the `markers` table on startup.

**Fix:** Changed the timeout to 30 minutes in [tracks.go:396](pkg/database/tracks.go#L396) — this is a one-time background maintenance task, so a long timeout is fine and won't block user requests (it runs in its own goroutine and uses the serialized pipeline's `WorkloadGeneral` lane).

Is tis corrcet?

> AGENT

Two issues:

**1. Syntax is wrong.** The `:?` operator means "fail with this message if unset" — it does **not** set a default. If `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` is already in your environment it works, but if not it will print the key as an error and exit. To use the hardcoded value as a fallback, it should be `:-`:

```bash
export ANTHROPIC_API_KEY="${ANTHROPIC_API_KEY:-sk-ant-api03-...}"
```

**2. Your API key is now visible in this screenshot.** You should rotate it at console.anthropic.com — treat any key that's appeared in a screenshot, chat, or log as compromised.

6024				}
6025				// Skip imports before start date (too old)

```
POST /api/admin/import-from-safecast?password=xxx
{"start_date": "YYYY-MM-DD", "end_date": "YYYY-MM-DD"}
```

So to import track 32558 today, you'd need to find its creation date from `api.safecast.org/en-US/bgeigie_imports/32558.json` and use that as the date range. Perfect! Now I have all the information I need. Let me compile a comprehensive report:

## Summary: Uploads Table Schema, Structs, and Data Flow

### 1. **Uploads Table Schema** (Database Columns)

The `uploads` table is created in `/home/rob/Documents/Safecast/safecast-new-map/pkg/database/database.go` with the following columns:

- `id` (BIGINT PRIMARY KEY / INTEGER PRIMARY KEY)
- `filename` (TEXT NOT NULL)
- `file_type` (TEXT)
- `track_id` (TEXT)
- `file_size` (BIGINT / INTEGER)
- `upload_ip` (TEXT)
- `created_at` (TIMESTAMPTZ / BIGINT)
- `recording_date` (TIMESTAMPTZ / BIGINT) — *Earliest marker date from the track*
- `source` (TEXT) — *Import source identifier (e.g., "safecast-api", "user-upload")*
- `source_id` (TEXT) — *External reference ID (e.g., Safecast import ID)*
- `source_url` (TEXT) — *Source file URL (e.g., S3 URL)*
- `user_id` (TEXT) — *External Safecast user ID*
- `username` (TEXT) — *Username from source (fetched from Safecast API)*
- `internal_user_id` (TEXT) — *Internal user ID from users table (for authenticated uploads)*
- `detector` (TEXT) — *Detector info from track_statistics*
- `name` (VARCHAR) — *Admin-editable display name (added by migration)*
- `notes` (VARCHAR) — *Admin notes (added by migration)*

**Indexes:**
- `idx_uploads_track_id`
- `idx_uploads_created_at`
- `idx_uploads_user_id`
- `idx_uploads_username`
- `idx_uploads_internal_user_id`
- `idx_uploads_detector` and `idx_uploads_detector_lower` (case-insensitive)

### 2. **Upload Struct Definition**

Located in `/home/rob/Documents/Safecast/safecast-new-map/pkg/database/uploads.go`:

```go
type Upload struct {
    ID             int64  `json:"id"`
    Filename       string `json:"filename"`
    FileType       string `json:"fileType"`
    TrackID        string `json:"trackID"`
    FileSize       int64  `json:"fileSize"`
    UploadIP       string `json:"uploadIP"`
    CreatedAt      int64  `json:"createdAt"`
    RecordingDate  int64  `json:"recordingDate,omitempty"` // Earliest marker date for this track
    Source         string `json:"source,omitempty"`         // Import source (e.g., "safecast-api", "user-upload")
    SourceID       string `json:"sourceID,omitempty"`       // External reference ID (e.g., Safecast import ID)
    SourceURL      string `json:"sourceURL,omitempty"`      // Source file URL (e.g., S3 URL)
    UserID         string `json:"userID,omitempty"`         // User ID from source (e.g., Safecast user ID)
    Username       string `json:"username,omitempty"`       // Username from source (fetched from API)
    InternalUserID string `json:"internalUserID,omitempty"` // Internal user ID from users table
    Detector       string `json:"detector,omitempty"`       // Detector info from track_statistics
    Name           string `json:"name,omitempty"`           // Display name (admin-editable)
    Notes          string `json:"notes,omitempty"`          // Admin notes
}
```

### 4. **Data Flow: SafecastImport → Database Store**

The `adminImportFromSafecastHandler` in `/home/rob/Documents/Safecast/safecast-new-map/main.go` (around line 6074+) calls:

**Step 1:** Download and fetch username
```go
// Line 6103: Download file
content, filename, err := safecastfetcher.DownloadLogFile(ctx, imp.SourceURL)

// Line 6117-6122: Fetch username from Safecast API
username := ""
if imp.UserID > 0 {
    user, err := client.FetchUser(ctx, imp.UserID)
    if err != nil {
        // log error
    } else if user != nil {
        username = user.Name
    }
}
```

**Step 2:** Call importer function (ImportSafecastFile)
```go
// Line 6132-6143
result, err := safecastfetcher.ImportSafecastFile(
    ctx,
    content,
    filename,
    int64(imp.ID),           // Safecast import ID
    imp.SourceURL,
    fmt.Sprintf("%d", imp.UserID), // userID as string
    username,                // Fetched from API
    db,
    *dbType,
    nil, // Use default importer
)
```

**Step 3:** In main.go's importerFunc (around line 8680-8698), the Upload struct is populated and stored:

```go
upload := database.Upload{
    Filename:      filename,                              // From download
    FileType:      ".log",                                // Hardcoded
    TrackID:       finalTrackID,                          // From import result
    FileSize:      int64(len(fileContent)),               // Computed
    UploadIP:      "safecast-api",                        // Hardcoded
    CreatedAt:     time.Now().Unix(),                     // Current time
    RecordingDate: recordingDate,                         // Earliest marker date from log
    Source:        safecastfetcher.SourceTypeSafecastAPI, // "safecast-api"
    SourceID:      fmt.Sprintf("%d", safecastImportID),   // Safecast import ID as string
    SourceURL:     sourceURL,                             // From SafecastImport.SourceURL
    UserID:        userID,                                // Safecast user ID (string)
    Username:      username,                              // From FetchUser API call
    Detector:      detector,                              // From track statistics
}

if _, err := db.InsertUpload(ctx, upload); err != nil {
    return finalTrackID, markerCount, fmt.Errorf("record upload: %w", err)
}
```

The **InsertUpload** method in `/home/rob/Documents/Safecast/safecast-new-map/pkg/database/uploads.go` (line 32-102) then inserts only these **14 fields into the database**:
- filename, file_type, track_id, file_size, upload_ip, created_at, recording_date, source, source_id, source_url, user_id, username, internal_user_id (NULL for imports), detector

**The Upload struct fields `Name` and `Notes` are NOT populated by the importer** — they are added by subsequent migrations and must be updated by admin separately.

### 5. **Migration File Naming Convention**

Files in `/home/rob/Documents/Safecast/safecast-new-map/migrations/`:
- **Naming pattern:** `verb_noun_description.sql` (lowercase with underscores)
  - `add_user_id_column.sql`
  - `add_recording_date_column.sql`
  - `add_upload_metadata.sql`
  - `add_detector_index.sql`
  - `fix_recording_dates.sql`
  - `link_historical_uploads_to_users.sql`
  
- **Content pattern:** Each file contains SQL statements with comments explaining purpose, supports multiple database types (PostgreSQL, SQLite, DuckDB) with conditional syntax or comments

The migrations are executed by the application's schema initialization logic in `database.go`, which reads and applies all `.sql` files in the migrations directory.

62	// FetchApprovedImports queries the API for approved imports
63	// Parameters:
64	//   - uploadedAfter: ISO date string (YYYY-MM-DD) to get imports after this date (empty for no filter)
65	//   - page: pagination page number (1-indexed)
66	//   - newestFirst: if true, return newest imports first (desc order)
67	func (c *Client) FetchApprovedImports(ctx context.Context, uploadedAfter string, page int, newestFirst bool) ([]SafecastImport, error) {
68		// Build URL: GET /bgeigie_imports?status=approved&page=N
69		// Note: Alternative API requires Accept header instead of .json extension
70		u, err := url.Parse(fmt.Sprintf("%s/bgeigie_imports", c.baseURL))
71		if err != nil {
72			return nil, fmt.Errorf("parse base URL: %w", err)
73		}
74	
75		query := u.Query()
76		query.Set("status", "approved")
77		// Order by created_at - ascending for oldest first, descending for newest first
78		if newestFirst {
79			query.Set("order", "created_at desc")
80		} else {
81			query.Set("order", "created_at asc")
82		}
83		if uploadedAfter != "" {
84			query.Set("uploaded_after", uploadedAfter)
85		}
86		if page > 0 {
87			query.Set("page", fmt.Sprintf("%d", page))
88		}
89		u.RawQuery = query.Encode()
90	
91		// Create request with context
92		req, err := http.NewRequestWithContext(ctx, "GET", u.String(), nil)
93		if err != nil {
94			return nil, fmt.Errorf("create request: %w", err)
95		}
96	
97		// Set Accept header to get JSON response (required by alternative API)
98		req.Header.Set("Accept", "application/json")
99	
100		// Execute request
101		resp, err := c.httpClient.Do(req)
102		if err != nil {
103			return nil, fmt.Errorf("execute request: %w", err)
104		}
105		defer resp.Body.Close()
106	
107		if resp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {
108			return nil, fmt.Errorf("unexpected status code: %d", resp.StatusCode)
109		}
110	
111		// Parse JSON response
112		var rawImports []safecastImportRaw
113		if err := json.NewDecoder(resp.Body).Decode(&rawImports); err != nil {
114			return nil, fmt.Errorf("decode response: %w", err)
115		}
116	
117		// Convert to SafecastImport (flatten source.url)
118		imports := make([]SafecastImport, 0, len(rawImports))
119		for _, raw := range rawImports {
120			imports = append(imports, SafecastImport{
121				ID:                raw.ID,
122				SourceURL:         raw.Source.URL,
123				UserID:            raw.UserID,
124				Approved:          raw.Approved,
125				CreatedAt:         raw.CreatedAt,
126				UpdatedAt:         raw.UpdatedAt,
127				MeasurementsCount: raw.MeasurementsCount,
128				MD5Sum:            raw.MD5Sum,
129				Name:              raw.Name,
130				Status:            raw.Status,
131			})
132		}
133	
134		return imports, nil
135	}
136

138	type SafecastUser struct {
139		ID   int64  `json:"id"`
140		Name string `json:"name"`
141	}

143	// FetchUser queries the API for user information by ID
144	func (c *Client) FetchUser(ctx context.Context, userID int64) (*SafecastUser, error) {
145		// Build URL: GET /users/:id.json
146		u := fmt.Sprintf("%s/users/%d.json", c.baseURL, userID)
147

149		req, err := http.NewRequestWithContext(ctx, "GET", u, nil)

152		}
153

157			return nil, fmt.Errorf("execute request: %w", err)
158		}
159		defer resp.Body.Close()
160	
161		if resp.StatusCode == http.StatusNotFound {
162			return nil, nil // User not found

164	
165		if resp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {
166			return nil, fmt.Errorf("unexpected status code: %d", resp.StatusCode)
167		}
168

172			return nil, fmt.Errorf("decode response: %w", err)
173		}
174	
175		return &user, nil
176	}

3	import (
4		"context"
5		"database/sql"

8	)
9	
10	// Upload represents a file upload record for tracking purposes.
11	type Upload struct {
12		ID            int64  `json:"id"`
13		Filename      string `json:"filename"`
14		FileType      string `json:"fileType"`
15		TrackID       string `json:"trackID"`
16		FileSize      int64  `json:"fileSize"`
17		UploadIP      string `json:"uploadIP"`
18		CreatedAt     int64  `json:"createdAt"`
19		RecordingDate int64  `json:"recordingDate,omitempty"` // Earliest marker date for this track
20		Source        string `json:"source,omitempty"`        // Import source (e.g., "safecast-api", "user-upload")
21		SourceID      string `json:"sourceID,omitempty"`      // External reference ID (e.g., Safecast import ID)
22		SourceURL     string `json:"sourceURL,omitempty"`     // Source file URL (e.g., S3 URL)
23		UserID         string `json:"userID,omitempty"`         // User ID from source (e.g., Safecast user ID)
24		Username       string `json:"username,omitempty"`       // Username from source (fetched from API)
25		InternalUserID string `json:"internalUserID,omitempty"` // Internal user ID from users table (for authenticated uploads)
26		Detector       string `json:"detector,omitempty"`       // Detector info from track_statistics
27		Name           string `json:"name,omitempty"`           // Display name (admin-editable)
28		Notes          string `json:"notes,omitempty"`          // Admin notes
29	}
30	
31	// InsertUpload records a file upload in the uploads table.
32	func (db *Database) InsertUpload(ctx context.Context, upload Upload) (int64, error) {
33		createdAt := upload.CreatedAt
34		if createdAt == 0 {
35			createdAt = time.Now().Unix()
36		}
37	
38		var query string
39		var args []interface{}
40		var id int64
41	
42		switch db.Driver {
43		case "pgx":
44			query = `
45				INSERT INTO uploads (filename, file_type, track_id, file_size, upload_ip, created_at, recording_date, source, source_id, source_url, user_id, username, internal_user_id, detector)
46				VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, to_timestamp($6), to_timestamp($7), $8, $9, $10, $11, $12, $13, $14)

104	// GetUploads retrieves upload records, ordered by most recent first.
105	// If userID is not empty, only returns uploads from that user.
106	func (db *Database) GetUploads(ctx context.Context, limit int, userID string) ([]Upload, error) {
107		return db.GetUploadsPaginated(ctx, limit, 0, userID, "")
108	}

OR COALESCE(u.source_id, '') LIKE ? OR COALESCE(u.detector, '') LIKE ? OR CAST(u.id AS TEXT) LIKE ? OR strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S', u.recording_date, 'unixepoch') LIKE ?)")

6124	
6125					// Import using the importer function from the fetcher
6126					mu.Lock()
6127					sendProgress(fmt.Sprintf("Processing %d/%d: Worker %d importing #%d...", processed+1, len(allImports), workerID, imp.ID), imported, skipped, errors, len(allImports))
6128					mu.Unlock()
6129	
6130					// Use the efficient batch processing importer from safecast-fetcher package
6131					// This uses the same optimized code path as backfill command
6132					result, err := safecastfetcher.ImportSafecastFile(
6133						ctx,
6134						content,
6135						filename,
6136						int64(imp.ID),
6137						imp.SourceURL,
6138						fmt.Sprintf("%d", imp.UserID),
6139						username,
6140						db,
6141						*dbType,
6142						nil, // Use default importer
6143					)
6144	
6145					if err != nil {
6146						log.Printf("[admin-import] worker %d: import #%d: import failed: %v", workerID, imp.ID, err)
6147						mu.Lock()
6148						errors++
6149						processed++
6150						sendProgress(fmt.Sprintf("Processing %d/%d: Error importing #%d", processed, len(allImports), imp.ID), imported, skipped, errors, len(allImports))
6151						mu.Unlock()
6152						continue
6153					}
6154	
6155					mu.Lock()
6156					imported++
6157					processed++
6158					log.Printf("[admin-import] worker %d: import #%d: success (track %s, %d markers)", workerID, imp.ID, result.TrackID, result.MarkerCount)
6159					sendProgress(fmt.Sprintf("Processing %d/%d: Worker %d imported #%d ✓ (%d markers)", processed, len(allImports), workerID, imp.ID, result.MarkerCount), imported, skipped, errors, len(allImports))
6160					mu.Unlock()
6161				}
6162			}(w)
6163		}
6164	
6165		// Send all jobs to workers
6166		for i, imp := range allImports {
6167			jobs <- importJob{index: i, imp: imp}
6168		}
6169		close(jobs)
6170	
6171		// Wait for all workers to complete
6172		wg.Wait()
6173

1	package safecastfetcher
2	
3	import (
4		"context"
5		"fmt"
6	
7		"safecast-new-map/pkg/database"
8	)
9	
10	// ImporterFunc is a function type that handles importing a bGeigie file
11	// This function is expected to be provided by the main application
12	// It should parse the file, store markers, and record the upload
13	type ImporterFunc func(
14		ctx context.Context,
15		fileContent []byte,
16		filename string,
17		safecastImportID int64,
18		sourceURL string,
19		userID string,
20		username string,
21		db *database.Database,
22		dbType string,
23	) (trackID string, markerCount int, err error)
24	
25	// DefaultImporter provides access to the default import functionality
26	// This will be set by the main application to avoid circular dependencies
27	var DefaultImporter ImporterFunc
28	
29	// ImportResult contains the results of an import operation
30	type ImportResult struct {
31		TrackID      string
32		MarkerCount  int
33		Filename     string
34		ImportID     int64
35		Error        error
36	}
37	
38	// ImportSafecastFile imports a downloaded log file using the provided importer function
39	func ImportSafecastFile(
40		ctx context.Context,
41		fileContent []byte,
42		filename string,
43		safecastImportID int64,
44		sourceURL string,
45		userID string,
46		username string,
47		db *database.Database,
48		dbType string,
49		importer ImporterFunc,
50	) (ImportResult, error) {
51		result := ImportResult{
52			Filename: filename,
53			ImportID: safecastImportID,
54		}
55	
56		// Use provided importer or default
57		importFunc := importer
58		if importFunc == nil {
59			importFunc = DefaultImporter
60		}
61		if importFunc == nil {
62			return result, fmt.Errorf("no importer function available")
63		}
64	
65		// Execute import
66		trackID, markerCount, err := importFunc(ctx, fileContent, filename, safecastImportID, sourceURL, userID, username, db, dbType)
67		if err != nil {
68			result.Error = err
69			return result, fmt.Errorf("import failed: %w", err)
70		}
71	
72		result.TrackID = trackID
73		result.MarkerCount = markerCount
74	
75		return result, nil
76	}
77

1	// register.go wires all HTTP routes: web/API handlers plus auth and admin endpoints.
2	package httpapi
3	
4	import (
5		"fmt"
6		"net/http"
7		"strconv"
8		"strings"
9	
10		"safecast-new-map/pkg/auth"
11		"safecast-new-map/pkg/database"
12	)
13	
14	// routeRegistrar is implemented by both Server (web) and Handler (core API).
15	type routeRegistrar interface {
16		Register(*http.ServeMux)
17	}
18	
19	// RegisterConfig holds everything needed to register all API and web routes.
20	// Fill WebServer (from NewWebServer) and APIHandler (from NewHandler); optionally
21	// set AuthManager, AdminPassword, and the admin handler funcs for protected routes.
22	type RegisterConfig struct {
23		WebServer  routeRegistrar
24		APIHandler routeRegistrar
25	
26		AuthManager   *auth.Manager
27		DB            *database.Database
28		AdminPassword string
29	
30		// Admin handlers; each is guarded by optional auth or admin password.
31		AdminUploadsHandler              http.HandlerFunc
32		AdminTracksHandler               http.HandlerFunc
33		AdminBackfillHandler             http.HandlerFunc
34		AdminBackfillCountriesHandler    http.HandlerFunc
35		AdminDeleteTrackHandler          http.HandlerFunc
36		AdminDeleteMultipleTracksHandler http.HandlerFunc
37		AdminImportFromSafecastHandler   http.HandlerFunc
38		AdminUpdateTrackHandler          http.HandlerFunc
39		AdminUpdateUploadHandler         http.HandlerFunc
40		AdminImportSafecastMetaHandler   http.HandlerFunc
41		AdminCacheHandler                http.HandlerFunc
42	
43		Logf func(string, ...any)
44	}
45	
46	// Register attaches all routes to mux: first web and core API routes from the
47	// registrars, then auth and admin routes from RegisterConfig.
48	func Register(mux *http.ServeMux, cfg RegisterConfig) {
49		if cfg.WebServer != nil {
50			cfg.WebServer.Register(mux)
51		}
52		if cfg.APIHandler != nil {
53			cfg.APIHandler.Register(mux)
54		}
55		registerAuthAndAdminRoutes(mux, cfg)
56	}
57	
58	// registerAuthAndAdminRoutes adds /api/auth/*, /api/user/*, and /api/admin/* routes.
59	func registerAuthAndAdminRoutes(mux *http.ServeMux, cfg RegisterConfig) {
60		if mux == nil {
61			return
62		}
63		if cfg.AuthManager != nil {
64			mux.HandleFunc("/api/auth/register", cfg.AuthManager.RegisterHandler)
65			mux.HandleFunc("/api/auth/login", cfg.AuthManager.LoginHandler)
66			mux.HandleFunc("/api/auth/logout", cfg.AuthManager.LogoutHandler)
67			mux.HandleFunc("/api/auth/forgot-password", cfg.AuthManager.ForgotPasswordHandler)
68			mux.HandleFunc("/api/auth/reset-password", cfg.AuthManager.ResetPasswordHandler)
69			mux.HandleFunc("/api/auth/verify-email", cfg.AuthManager.VerifyEmailHandler)
70			mux.HandleFunc("/api/user/profile", cfg.AuthManager.RequireAuth(cfg.AuthManager.ProfileHandler))
71			mux.HandleFunc("/api/user/change-password", cfg.AuthManager.RequireAuth(cfg.AuthManager.ChangePasswordHandler))
72			mux.HandleFunc("/api/user/uploads", cfg.AuthManager.RequireAuth(func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
73				handleUserUploads(w, r, cfg)
74			}))
75	
76			mux.HandleFunc("/api/admin/users", cfg.AuthManager.OptionalAuth(func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
77				if !checkAdminAccess(w, r, cfg.AdminPassword) {
78					return
79				}
80				cfg.AuthManager.AdminListUsersHandler(w, r)
81			}))
82			mux.HandleFunc("/api/admin/users/create", cfg.AuthManager.OptionalAuth(func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
83				if !checkAdminAccess(w, r, cfg.AdminPassword) {
84					return
85				}
86				cfg.AuthManager.AdminCreateUserHandler(w, r)
87			}))
88			mux.HandleFunc("/api/admin/users/", cfg.AuthManager.OptionalAuth(func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
89				if !checkAdminAccess(w, r, cfg.AdminPassword) {
90					return
91				}
92				switch r.Method {
93				case http.MethodPut, http.MethodPatch:
94					cfg.AuthManager.AdminUpdateUserHandler(w, r)
95				case http.MethodDelete:
96					cfg.AuthManager.AdminDeleteUserHandler(w, r)
97				case http.MethodPost:
98					if strings.HasSuffix(r.URL.Path, "/reset-password") {
99						cfg.AuthManager.AdminResetUserPasswordHandler(w, r)
100					} else if strings.HasSuffix(r.URL.Path, "/regenerate-api-key") {
101						cfg.AuthManager.AdminRegenerateAPIKeyHandler(w, r)
102					} else {
103						http.Error(w, "Not found", http.StatusNotFound)
104					}
105				default:
106					http.Error(w, "Method not allowed", http.StatusMethodNotAllowed)
107				}
108			}))
109		}
110	
111		guard := func(next http.HandlerFunc) http.HandlerFunc {
112			if next == nil {
113				return nil
114			}
115			if cfg.AuthManager != nil {
116				return cfg.AuthManager.OptionalAuth(next)
117			}
118			return next
119		}
120	
121		registerOptional := func(path string, handler http.HandlerFunc) {
122			if handler == nil {
123				return
124			}
125			mux.HandleFunc(path, guard(handler))
126		}
127	
128		registerOptional("/api/admin/uploads", cfg.AdminUploadsHandler)
129		registerOptional("/api/admin/tracks", cfg.AdminTracksHandler)

134		registerOptional("/api/admin/import-from-safecast", cfg.AdminImportFromSafecastHandler)
135		registerOptional("/api/admin/tracks/update", cfg.AdminUpdateTrackHandler)

138		registerOptional("/api/admin/cache", cfg.AdminCacheHandler)
139	}

144			return true

147			return true
148		}

150		return false

271	// DeleteTrack removes all data associated with a track ID.
272	// This includes markers, spectra, and upload records.
273	func (db *Database) DeleteTrack(ctx context.Context, trackID string) error {
274		// Delete from spectra first (via CASCADE from markers)
275		// Then delete markers, tracks, and upload records
276	
277		queries := []string{
278			"DELETE FROM markers WHERE trackID = ?",
279			"DELETE FROM tracks WHERE trackID = ?",
280			"DELETE FROM uploads WHERE track_id = ?",
281		}
282	
283		if db.Driver == "pgx" || db.Driver == "duckdb" {
284			queries = []string{
285				"DELETE FROM markers WHERE trackID = $1",
286				"DELETE FROM tracks WHERE trackID = $1",
287				"DELETE FROM uploads WHERE track_id = $1",
288			}
289		}
290	
291		for _, query := range queries {
292			_, err := db.DB.ExecContext(ctx, query, trackID)
293			if err != nil {
294				return fmt.Errorf("delete track data: %w", err)
295			}
296		}
297	
298		return nil
299	}

{
  "replace_all": false,

  "old_string": "// SafecastUser represents a user from api.safecast.org",
  "new_string": "// FetchImportByID fetches a single bgeigie import by its numeric ID.\nfunc (c *Client) FetchImportByID(ctx context.Context, id int64) (*SafecastImport, error) {\n\tu := fmt.Sprintf(\"%s/bgeigie_imports/%d.json\", c.baseURL, id)\n\n\treq, err := http.NewRequestWithContext(ctx, \"GET\", u, nil)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"create request: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\treq.Header.Set(\"Accept\", \"application/json\")\n\n\tresp, err := c.httpClient.Do(req)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"execute request: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tdefer resp.Body.Close()\n\n\tif resp.StatusCode == http.StatusNotFound {\n\t\treturn nil, nil\n\t}\n\tif resp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"unexpected status code: %d\", resp.StatusCode)\n\t}\n\n\tvar raw safecastImportRaw\n\tif err := json.NewDecoder(resp.Body).Decode(&raw); err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"decode response: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\timp := SafecastImport{\n\t\tID:                raw.ID,\n\t\tSourceURL:         raw.Source.URL,\n\t\tUserID:            raw.UserID,\n\t\tApproved:          raw.Approved,\n\t\tCreatedAt:         raw.CreatedAt,\n\t\tUpdatedAt:         raw.UpdatedAt,\n\t\tMeasurementsCount: raw.MeasurementsCount,\n\t\tMD5Sum:            raw.MD5Sum,\n\t\tName:              raw.Name,\n\t\tComment:           raw.Comment,\n\t\tStatus:            raw.Status,\n\t}\n\treturn &imp, nil\n}\n\n// SafecastUser represents a user from api.safecast.org"
}

  "old_string": "// ImportSafecastFile imports a downloaded log file using the provided importer function\nfunc ImportSafecastFile(\n\tctx context.Context,\n\tfileContent []byte,\n\tfilename string,\n\tsafecastImportID int64,\n\tsourceURL string,\n\tuserID string,\n\tusername string,\n\tdb *database.Database,\n\tdbType string,\n\timporter ImporterFunc,\n) (ImportResult, error) {\n\tresult := ImportResult{\n\t\tFilename: filename,\n\t\tImportID: safecastImportID,\n\t}\n\n\t// Use provided importer or default\n\timportFunc := importer\n\tif importFunc == nil {\n\t\timportFunc = DefaultImporter\n\t}\n\tif importFunc == nil {\n\t\treturn result, fmt.Errorf(\"no importer function available\")\n\t}\n\n\t// Execute import\n\ttrackID, markerCount, err := importFunc(ctx, fileContent, filename, safecastImportID, sourceURL, userID, username, db, dbType)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tresult.Error = err\n\t\treturn result, fmt.Errorf(\"import failed: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tresult.TrackID = trackID\n\tresult.MarkerCount = markerCount\n\n\treturn result, nil\n}",
  "new_string": "// ImportSafecastFile imports a downloaded log file using the provided importer function\nfunc ImportSafecastFile(\n\tctx context.Context,\n\tfileContent []byte,\n\tfilename string,\n\tsafecastImportID int64,\n\tsourceURL string,\n\tuserID string,\n\tusername string,\n\tcomment string,\n\tdb *database.Database,\n\tdbType string,\n\timporter ImporterFunc,\n) (ImportResult, error) {\n\tresult := ImportResult{\n\t\tFilename: filename,\n\t\tImportID: safecastImportID,\n\t}\n\n\t// Use provided importer or default\n\timportFunc := importer\n\tif importFunc == nil {\n\t\timportFunc = DefaultImporter\n\t}\n\tif importFunc == nil {\n\t\treturn result, fmt.Errorf(\"no importer function available\")\n\t}\n\n\t// Execute import\n\ttrackID, markerCount, err := importFunc(ctx, fileContent, filename, safecastImportID, sourceURL, userID, username, comment, db, dbType)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tresult.Error = err\n\t\treturn result, fmt.Errorf(\"import failed: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tresult.TrackID = trackID\n\tresult.MarkerCount = markerCount\n\n\treturn result, nil\n}"
}

exists\", imp.ID),\n\t\t})\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\tcontent, filename, err := safecastfetcher.DownloadLogFile(ctx, imp.SourceURL)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\thttp.Error(w, fmt.Sprintf(\"download: %v\", err), http.StatusBadGateway)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\tusername := \"\"\n\tif imp.UserID > 0 {\n\t\tuser, err := client.FetchUser(ctx, imp.UserID)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\tlog.Printf(\"[admin-import-by-id] imp.UserID),\n\t\tusername,\n\t\timp.Comment,\n\t\tdb,\n\t\t*dbType,\n\t\tnil,\n\t)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\thttp.Error(w, fmt.Sprintf(\"import failed: %v\", err), > AGENT

Clean build. Here's a summary of what was done:

**1. `comment` field everywhere**
- `SafecastImport.Comment` + `safecastImportRaw.Comment` — fetched from API JSON
- `Upload.Comment` — stored in DB
- `InsertUpload` — writes `comment` column (all 3 drivers)
- `GetUploadsPaginated` — selects + scans `comment`
- `migrations/add_comment_to_uploads.sql` — `ALTER TABLE uploads ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS comment TEXT`
- `ImporterFunc` and `ImportSafecastFile` — `comment string` param threaded through
- `importerFunc` closure in `cmd/unified-server/main.go` — passes `comment` to `Upload{}`

**2. New endpoint: `POST /api/admin/import-by-id`**
- Body: `{"id": 32558}`
- Returns `409 Conflict` if already imported, `404` if not found on Safecast API
- Returns JSON: `{"imported": true, "track_id": "...", "marker_count": N, "filename": "...", "comment": "..."}`

**Usage:**
```bash
curl -X POST "http://localhost:8765/api/admin/import-by-id?password=test123" \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"id": 32558}'
```

  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \

Unauthorized

5983	// formatFileSize converts bytes to human-readable format
5984	func formatFileSize(bytes int64) string {
5985		const unit = 1024
5986		if bytes < unit {
5987			return fmt.Sprintf("%d B", bytes)
5988		}
5989		div, exp := int64(unit), 0
5990		for n := bytes / unit; n >= unit; n /= unit {
5991			div *= unit
5992			exp++
5993		}
5994		return fmt.Sprintf("%.1f %cB", float64(bytes)/float64(div), "KMGTPE"[exp])
5995	}

> AGENT

Good. Now run the backfill:

```bash
curl -s -X POST "http://localhost:8765/api/admin/tracks/import-safecast?password=admin123" \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{}'
```

This will hit all uploads with `source='safecast-api'` that have a blank `comment` (or blank `name`), fetch each one from the Safecast API, and write `name` and `comment` back. It returns `{"ok":true,"updated":N}` when done.

Note: with 47k uploads this will take a while — it makes one API call per row. You may want to run it overnight.

> DEVELOPER

rob@rob-GS66-Stealth-10UG:~$ curl -s -X POST "http://localhost:8765/api/admin/tracks/import-safecast?password=admin123" \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{}'
unauthorized
rob@rob-GS66-Stealth-10UG:~$

> AGENT

The handler checks `req.Password` (from the JSON body), not the URL param. Pass it in the body:

```bash
curl -s -X POST "http://localhost:8765/api/admin/tracks/import-safecast" \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{"password":"admin123"}'
```

> DEVELOPER

Seems to run.. I can imagine it can take a while?

> AGENT

Yes, it's sequential — one HTTP request to api.safecast.org per upload, and you have ~47k rows with `source='safecast-api'`. At ~100-200ms per request that's potentially hours.

If you want to speed it up I can parallelize it with a worker pool (similar to how `adminImportFromSafecastHandler` works with 8 concurrent workers). Want me to do that?

> DEVELOPER

Yes.. Can we resume if it stops?

> AGENT

The resume is actually already handled — the WHERE clause only picks rows where `comment IS NULL OR comment = ''`, so rows already filled are skipped automatically. I just need to add the worker pool:

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_019SW42QMRUmFpniH6M4oy1W
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/home/rob/Documents/Safecast/safecast-new-map/cmd/unified-server/main.go",
  "old_string": "\tupdated := 0\n\tclient := &http.Client{Timeout: 10 * time.Second}\n\tfor _, c := range candidates {\n\t\tapiURL := \"https://api.safecast.org/bgeigie_imports/\" + c.SourceID + \".json\"\n\t\tresp, err := client.Get(apiURL)\n\t\tif err != nil || resp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {\n\t\t\tif resp != nil {\n\t\t\t\tresp.Body.Close()\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tvar meta struct {\n\t\t\tName    string `json:\"name\"`\n\t\t\tComment string `json:\"comment\"`\n\t\t}\n\t\tif err := json.NewDecoder(resp.Body).Decode(&meta); err != nil || meta.Name == \"\" {\n\t\t\tresp.Body.Close()\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tresp.Body.Close()\n\n\t\tif _, err := db.DB.ExecContext(r.Context(),\n\t\t\t`UPDATE uploads SET name = $1, comment = $2 WHERE track_id = $3`,\n\t\t\tmeta.Name, meta.Comment, c.TrackID,\n\t\t); err == nil {\n\t\t\tupdated++\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tw.Header().Set(\"Content-Type\", \"application/json\")\n\tjson.NewEncoder(w).Encode(map[string]any{\"ok\": true, \"updated\": updated})",
  "new_string": "\tconst numWorkers = 16\n\ttype result struct{ updated bool }\n\tjobs := make(chan candidate, len(candidates))\n\tresults := make(chan result, len(candidates))\n\n\thttpClient := &http.Client{Timeout: 10 * time.Second}\n\tfor i := 0; i < numWorkers; i++ {\n\t\tgo func() {\n\t\t\tfor c := range jobs {\n\t\t\t\tapiURL := \"https://api.safecast.org/bgeigie_imports/\" + c.SourceID + \".json\"\n\t\t\t\tresp, err := httpClient.Get(apiURL)\n\t\t\t\tif err != nil || resp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {\n\t\t\t\t\tif resp != nil {\n\t\t\t\t\t\tresp.Body.Close()\n\t\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\t\tresults <- result{}\n\t\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tvar meta struct {\n\t\t\t\t\tName    string `json:\"name\"`\n\t\t\t\t\tComment string `json:\"comment\"`\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tdecodeErr := json.NewDecoder(resp.Body).Decode(&meta)\n\t\t\t\tresp.Body.Close()\n\t\t\t\tif decodeErr != nil || meta.Name == \"\" {\n\t\t\t\t\tresults <- result{}\n\t\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\t_, err = db.DB.ExecContext(r.Context(),\n\t\t\t\t\t`UPDATE uploads SET name = $1, comment = $2 WHERE track_id = $3`,\n\t\t\t\t\tmeta.Name, meta.Comment, c.TrackID,\n\t\t\t\t)\n\t\t\t\tresults <- result{updated: err == nil}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}()\n\t}\n\n\tfor _, c := range candidates {\n\t\tjobs <- c\n\t}\n\tclose(jobs)\n\n\tupdated := 0\n\tfor range candidates {\n\t\tif r := <-results; r.updated {\n\t\t\tupdated++\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tw.Header().Set(\"Content-Type\", \"application/json\")\n\tjson.NewEncoder(w).Encode(map[string]any{\"ok\": true, \"updated\": updated, \"total\": len(candidates)})"
}
```

It helps you think but <strong>does not replace common sense</strong>.</li></ul></section><hr /><section style=\"margin:0 0 12px; display:flex; gap:10px; align-items:flex-start;\"><div style=\"min-width:84px; padding:8px 10px; border-radius:10px; background:#1b5e20; color:#fff; text-align:center; font-weight:700;\">🟩<br />0–11</div><div><h2 style=\"margin:0;\">Green zone</h2><h3 style=\"margin:4px 0; font-weight:600;\">\"Generally safe — but stay attentive\"</h3><p style=\"margin:4px 0;\"><strong>0–11 microroentgens per hour</strong> — close to natural background.</p><ul style=\"margin:4px 0 0 18px;\"><li>you can usually live here permanently</li><li>drink the water</li><li>grow and eat local produce</li><li>this <strong>does not mean radiation is absent</strong></li><li>it means levels are <strong>about what the body is used to</strong></li><li>⚠ hidden risks still exist: indoor radon; radioactive impurities in water; accumulation of radionuclides in food and tobacco</li></ul></div></section><hr /><section style=\"margin:0 0 12px; display:flex; gap:10px; align-items:flex-start;\"><div style=\"min-width:84px; padding:8px 10px; border-radius:10px; background:#f9a825; color:#111; text-align:center; font-weight:700;\">🟨<br />11–30</div><div><h2 style=\"margin:0;\">Yellow zone</h2><h3 style=\"margin:4px 0; font-weight:600;\">\"Pay attention and find out why\"</h3><p style=\"margin:4px 0;\"><strong>11–30 microroentgens per hour</strong>. Usually no immediate danger, but a yellow patch inside green is <strong>a reason to pause</strong>.</p><ul style=\"margin:4px 0 0 18px;\"><li>possible causes: radon; alpha or beta contamination the dosimeter only partially sees; localised sources</li><li>sensible steps: test water and food; identify the source; measure indoor radon</li><li>harm <strong>can build up over time</strong>, even if you feel fine now</li></ul></div></section><hr /><section style=\"margin:0 0 12px; display:flex; gap:10px; align-items:flex-start;\"><div style=\"min-width:84px; padding:8px 10px; border-radius:10px; background:#c62828; color:#fff; text-align:center; font-weight:700;\">🟥<br />30–100</div><div><h2 style=\"margin:0;\">Red zone</h2><h3 style=\"margin:4px 0; font-weight:600;\">\"Without checks, it is not worth the risk\"</h3><p style=\"margin:4px 0;\"><strong>30–100 microroentgens per hour</strong> — no longer a normal level.</p><ul style=\"margin:4px 0 0 18px;\"><li>avoid: living here permanently; regularly drinking local water; relying on local food</li><li>possible causes: man-made radionuclides; contaminated soil; high radon in homes and water</li><li>recommended: drink water <strong>only after testing</strong>; eat food <strong>after checks</strong>; live here <strong>only with full awareness of the risks</strong></li></ul></div></section><hr /><section style=\"margin:0 0 12px; display:flex; gap:10px; align-items:flex-start;\"><div style=\"min-width:84px; padding:8px 10px; border-radius:10px; background:#111; color:#fff; text-align:center; font-weight:700;\">⬛<br />100+</div><div><h2 style=\"margin:0;\">Black zone</h2><h3 style=\"margin:4px 0; font-weight:600;\">\"Extended exposure is unsafe\"</h3><p style=\"margin:4px 0;\"><strong>Over 100 microroentgens per hour</strong> — a serious level of exposure.</p><ul style=\"margin:4px 0 0 18px;\"><li>risk fronts: external irradiation; radionuclides entering the body; potential skin damage</li><li>food and water may contain hazardous substances even if they look and smell normal</li><li>🔹 exceptions (brief skin exposure): air travel; high-altitude locations</li></ul></div></section><hr /><section style=\"margin:0 0 12px;\"><h2>🔍 What a dosimeter shows — and what it misses</h2><div style=\"display:flex; gap:12px;\"><div style=\"flex:1;\"><h3 style=\"margin:4px 0;\">Typically detected:</h3><ul style=\"margin:4px 0 0 18px;\"><li>gamma radiation</li><li>sometimes part of beta radiation</li></ul></div><div style=\"flex:1;\"><h3 style=\"margin:4px 0;\">Often missed:</h3><ul style=\"margin:4px 0 0 18px;\"><li>alpha radiation (especially dangerous once inside the body)</li><li>radon — a gas we breathe and drink with water</li><li>tritium in water and air</li><li>radionuclides in food</li><li>neutrons (rare, but extremely dangerous)</li><li>👉 a \"green\" reading <strong>does not equal full safety</strong>.</li></ul></div></div></section><hr /><section style=\"margin:0 0 12px;\"><h2>🧬 How radiation actually enters the body</h2><div style=\"display:flex; gap:12px;\"><div style=\"flex:1;\"><h3 style=\"margin:4px 0;\">The most dangerous routes are quiet and long</h3><ul style=\"margin:4px 0 0 18px;\"><li><strong>Through breathing</strong> — radon, dust, smoke → lungs</li><li><strong>Through water</strong> — tritium and dissolved isotopes → whole body</li><li><strong>Through food</strong> — mushrooms, greens, milk → accumulation in tissues</li><li><strong>Through smoking</strong> — tobacco readily absorbs radionuclides</li></ul></div><div style=\"flex:1;\"><h3 style=\"margin:4px 0;\">Less dangerous:</h3><ul style=\"margin:4px 0 0 18px;\"><li>brief external exposure</li><li>one-off contact</li></ul></div></div></section><hr /><section style=\"margin:0 0 12px;\"><h2>⏳ Time is the main risk factor</h2><p style=\"margin:4px 0;\">Radiation is dangerous <strong>because of prolonged exposure</strong>. Moderate doses taken continuously can be more harmful than a short spike. The body can eliminate radionuclides, but with constant exposure and other stresses those systems <strong>get overloaded</strong>.</p></section><hr /><section style=\"margin:0 0 12px;\"><h2>👥 Who is affected more severely</h2><p style=\"margin:4px 0;\">Higher risk for: older people; those with chronic illnesses. What one body tolerates without consequences may cause serious problems for another.</p></section><hr /><section style=\"margin:0 0 12px;\"><h2>⚛️ Radioactive \"disguise\"</h2><p style=\"margin:4px 0;\">Some radionuclides resemble ordinary substances to the body: <strong>tritium</strong> — like water; <strong>carbon-14</strong> — like normal carbon; <strong>potassium-40</strong> — like potassium; <strong>strontium, radium</strong> — like calcium. Cells <strong>do not recognise the substitution</strong> and incorporate them. Damage and mutations are possible even when the dosimeter reads \"normal.\"</p></section><hr /><section style=\"margin:0;\"><h2>🗺️ Why a map should not be trusted blindly</h2><p style=\"margin:4px 0;\">Even a perfectly green map reflects <strong>only part of reality</strong>. Hidden factors are not shown. 👉 A map is a hint, not a pardon. You still need to think, check, and ask questions yourself.</p></section>",

  "new_string": "\t\thtml += `\n\t\t</tbody>\n\t</table>\n\t</div>`\n\t}\n\n\thtml += `\n\t<script>\n\t\t// Column resize handles\n\t\t(function() {\n\t\t\tlet resizing = false;\n\t\t\tdocument.querySelectorAll('#uploadsTable .resize-handle').forEach(handle => {\n\t\t\t\thandle.addEventListener('mousedown', function(e) {\n\t\t\t\t\te.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation();\n\t\t\t\t\tresizing = true;\n\t\t\t\t\tconst th = this.parentElement;\n\t\t\t\t\tconst startX = e.pageX, startWidth = th.offsetWidth;\n\t\t\t\t\tthis.classList.add('active');\n\t\t\t\t\tconst onMove = e => { th.style.width = Math.max(40, startWidth + (e.pageX - startX)) + 'px'; };\n\t\t\t\t\tconst onUp = () => {\n\t\t\t\t\t\tthis.classList.remove('active');\n\t\t\t\t\t\tdocument.removeEventListener('mousemove', onMove);\n\t\t\t\t\t\tdocument.removeEventListener('mouseup', onUp);\n\t\t\t\t\t\tsetTimeout(() => { resizing = false; }, 50);\n\t\t\t\t\t};\n\t\t\t\t\tdocument.addEventListener('mousemove', onMove);\n\t\t\t\t\tdocument.addEventListener('mouseup', onUp);\n\t\t\t\t});\n\t\t\t});\n\t\t\tdocument.querySelectorAll('#uploadsTable .sortable').forEach(th => {\n\t\t\t\tth.addEventListener('click', e => { if (resizing) e.stopImmediatePropagation(); });\n\t\t\t});\n\t\t})();\n\n\t\t// Apply theme from sessionStorage to match map preference"
}
